f:T658bf,<h2 id="es2024es15">ES2024(ES15)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2024es15"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p><strong>ES2024에 추가된 기능들</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://tc39.es/ecma262/">ECMAScript® 2024 Language Specification</a></p>
<p>ECMAScript 2024, the 15th edition, added facilities for resizing and transferring ArrayBuffers and SharedArrayBuffers; added a new RegExp <strong><code>/v</code></strong> flag for creating RegExps with more advanced features for working with sets of strings; and introduced the <strong><code>Promise.withResolvers</code></strong> convenience method for constructing Promises, the <strong><code>Object.groupBy</code></strong> and <strong><code>Map.groupBy</code></strong> methods for aggregating data, the <strong><code>Atomics.waitAsync</code></strong> method for asynchronously waiting for a change to shared memory, and the <strong><code>String.prototype.isWellFormed</code></strong> and <strong><code>String.prototype.toWellFormed</code></strong> methods for checking and ensuring that strings contain only well-formed Unicode.</p>
<p><strong>Promise.withResolvers</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-promise-with-resolvers">tc39/proposal-promise-with-resolvers</a></p>
<p><a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Promise/withResolvers">Promise.withResolvers() - JavaScript | MDN</a></p>
<p>Promise 확장 방법 중 하나로 새로운 <code>Promise</code> 인스턴스와 해당 Promise의 해결(resolve) 및 거부(reject) 함수를 반환합니다</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.withResolvers()다음 코드와 정확히 동일합니다</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> resolve, reject;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">res</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rej</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  resolve </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> res;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  reject </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> rej;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 스트림을 비동기 반복 가능으로 변환</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> function*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> readableToAsyncIterable</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">stream</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { promise, resolve, reject } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">withResolvers</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  stream.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">on</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'error'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(error));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  stream.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">on</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'end'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  stream.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">on</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'readable'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (stream.readable) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> promise;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> chunk;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ((chunk </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> stream.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">read</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">())) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> chunk;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    ({ promise, resolve, reject } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">withResolvers</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise가 아닌 생성자에서 withResolvers() 호출</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> NotPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">executor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // The "resolve" and "reject" functions behave nothing like the native</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // promise's, but Promise.withResolvers() just returns them, as is.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    executor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Resolved'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, value),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">      reason</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Rejected'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, reason),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.withResolvers.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">call</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(NotPromise);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hello'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>proposal-array-grouping</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-array-grouping">tc39/proposal-array-grouping</a></p>
<p>배열의 아이템을 객체/Map 형식으로 그룹화 해주는 함수</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// `Object.groupBy` groups items by arbitrary key.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// In this case, we're grouping by even/odd keys</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">groupBy</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(array, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">index</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'even'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'odd'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// =>  { odd: [1, 3, 5], even: [2, 4] }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// `Map.groupBy` returns items in a Map, and is useful for grouping</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// using an object key.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> odd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { odd: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> even</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> { even: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Map.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">groupBy</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(array, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">index</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> even </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> odd;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// =>  Map { {odd: true}: [1, 3, 5], {even: true}: [2, 4] }</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Atomics.waitAsync</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-atomics-wait-async">tc39/proposal-atomics-wait-async</a></p>
<p><a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/ko/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/Atomics/waitAsync">Atomics.waitAsync() - JavaScript | MDN</a></p>
<p><a href="http://hacks.mozilla.or.kr/2017/11/a-cartoon-intro-to-arraybuffers-and-sharedarraybuffers/">ArrayBuffer/SharedArrayBuffer</a></p>
<p>정적 메서드는 공유 메모리 위치에서 비동기적으로 대기하고 Promise를 반환하는 함수</p>
<ul>
<li>참고로 위 메소드는 Int32Array 혹은 BigInt64Array에서만 동작</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> sab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> SharedArrayBuffer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1024</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> int32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Int32Array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(sab);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 읽기 스레드가 0이 될 것으로 예상되는 위치 0에서 기대하며, 1000ms 대기합니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// result.value은 프로미스입니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> Atomics.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">waitAsync</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(int32, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// { async: true, value: Promise {&#x3C;pending>} }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 읽기 스레드 또는 다른 스레드에서 메모리 위치 0이 호출되고 이행 결과 "ok" 문자열을 확인</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Atomics.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">notify</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(int32, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// { async: true, value: Promise {&#x3C;fulfilled>: 'ok'} }</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>RegExp <code>/v</code> flag</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-regexp-v-flag">tc39/proposal-regexp-v-flag</a></p>
<p>정규표현식의 패턴 매칭을 통해 유니코드 이모지를 /u flag 값으로 검색이 가능하지만, 조합된 이모지 코드값으로는 검색이 가능하도록 /v flag 값 설정 추가</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Unicode defines a character property named “Emoji”.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> re</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#72F088;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">\p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">{Emoji}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Match an emoji that consists of just 1 code point:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">re.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'⚽'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// '\u26BD'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// → true ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Match an emoji that consists of multiple code points:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">re.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'👨🏾‍⚕️'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// '\u{1F468}\u{1F3FE}\u200D\u2695\uFE0F'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// → false ❌</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> re</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">^</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#72F088;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">\p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">{RGI_Emoji}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">$</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">v</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Match an emoji that consists of just 1 code point:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">re.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'⚽'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// '\u26BD'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// → true ✅</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Match an emoji that consists of multiple code points:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">re.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">test</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'👨🏾‍⚕️'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// '\u{1F468}\u{1F3FE}\u200D\u2695\uFE0F'</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// → true ✅</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>올바른 형식의 유니코드 문자열</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://exploringjs.com/js/book/ch_strings.html#qref-String.prototype.isWellFormed">문자열 메서드 .isWellFormed()</a>는 자바스크립트 문자열이 <em>올바른 형식</em>이고 <a href="https://exploringjs.com/js/book/ch_unicode.html#unicode-lone-surrogate">론 서로게이트(lone surrogate)</a>를 포함하고 있지 않은지 검사합니다.</li>
<li><a href="https://exploringjs.com/js/book/ch_strings.html#qref-String.prototype.isWellFormed">문자열 메서드 .toWellFormed()</a>는 론 서로게이트를 코드 유닛 0xFFFD로 교체한 문자열의 복사본을 반환합니다(0xFFFD는 같은 수의 코드 포인트를 나타내며 이름은 "대체 문자" 입니다). 따라서 결괏값은 올바른 형식입니다.</li>
</ul>
<p>surrogates 란?</p>
<ul>
<li>유니코드에서 surrogate는 기본 다국어 평면(Basic Multilingual Plane, BMP)에 속하지 않는 문자를 나타내기 위해 사용되는 코드 포인트입니다.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">high </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">surrogate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">U</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">D800</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ~</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> U</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">DBFF</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">log </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">surrogate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">U</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">DC00</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ~</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> U</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">DFFF</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>lone surrogate란?</p>
<ul>
<li>서로게이트 페어에서 하나만 남은 경우를 가리킨다. 고위 서로게이트가 있고, 저위 서로게이트가 없거나, 그 반대의 경우를 의미한다. 이러한 상황은 올바른 UTF-16 표현이 아니며, 유효한 문자를 나타내지 않는다.파일 전송이나 텍스트 처리 중에 문제가 발생할 수 있다.</li>
<li>unicode 표준은 이러한 상황을 처리하는 규칙을 제공하고 있다.</li>
</ul>
<p>surrogates 문자열을 유니코드 대체 문자 U+FFFD로 대체되는 <code>[String](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Global_Objects/String)</code>문자열을 반환 처리</p>
<ul>
<li>surrogates 문자열은 항상 <code>leading</code>과 <code>trailing</code>이 pair로 이루어져야 하지만, 쌍을 이루지 않을 경우에는 오류가 발생하고, 서버와 통신으로 문자열 전달 시 오류가 발생함으로 꼭 toWellFormed로 변경해서 보내야함.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> strings</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Lone leading surrogate</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  'ab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uD800</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  'ab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uD800</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">c'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Lone trailing surrogate</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uDFFF</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">ab'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  'c</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uDFFF</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">ab'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">  // Well-formed</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  'abc'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">  'ab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uD83D\uDE04</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">c'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> strings) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(str.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toWellFormed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Logs:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "ab�"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "ab�c"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "�ab"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "c�ab"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "abc"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "ab😄c"</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> illFormed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'https://example.com/search?q=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">\uD800</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">try</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  encodeURI</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(illFormed);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (e) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(e); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// URIError: URI malformed</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">encodeURI</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(illFormed.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toWellFormed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">())); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "https://example.com/search?q=%EF%BF%BD"</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es2023es14">ES2023(ES14)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2023es14"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p><strong>ES2023에 추가된 기능들</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://tc39.es/ecma262/">ECMAScript® 2024 Language Specification</a></p>
<p>ECMAScript 2023, the 14th edition, introduced the <code>toSorted</code>, <code>toReversed</code>, <code>with</code>, <code>findLast</code>, and <code>findLastIndex</code> methods on <code>Array.prototype</code> and <code>TypedArray.prototype</code>, as well as the <code>toSpliced</code> method on <code>Array.prototype</code>; added support for <code>#!</code> comments at the beginning of files to better facilitate executable ECMAScript files; and allowed the use of most Symbols as keys in weak collections.</p>
<p><strong>Array find from last</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-array-find-from-last">tc39/proposal-array-find-from-last</a></p>
<p>배열의 값을 뒤로부터 찾을 수 있는 기능이 추가되었습니다.</p>
<p>findLast, findLastIndex</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [{ value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }, { value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }, { value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }, { value: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">find</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> n.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// { value: 1 }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findIndex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> n.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ======== Before the proposal ===========</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// find</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array].</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">reverse</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findIndex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> n.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 42</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// should be -1, but 4</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// findIndex</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findLastIndex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> n.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">array.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">findLastIndex</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> n.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 42</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// -1</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><strong>Change Array by Copy</strong></p>
<p><a href="https://github.com/tc39/proposal-change-array-by-copy">tc39/proposal-change-array-by-copy</a></p>
<p>자바스크립트에서 배열을 변경 시 기존 배열을 얕은 복사하다보니 기존 배열이 변경되는 API들이 있는데요, 해당 API에 대한 깊은 복사(새로운 배열 객체)를 해주는 API들이 추가되었습니다.</p>
<p>함수형 프로그래밍의 불변성(Immutable) 개념이 적용된 사례인거 같네요</p>
<p>Array.prototype.toReversed() -> Array<br>
Array.prototype.toSorted(compareFn) -> Array<br>
Array.prototype.toSpliced(start, deleteCount, ...items) -> Array<br>
Array.prototype.with(index, value) -> Array<br>
TypedArray.prototype.toReversed() -> TypedArray<br>
TypedArray.prototype.toSorted(compareFn) -> TypedArray<br>
TypedArray.prototype.with(index, value) -> TypedArray<br></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>toReversed</strong></li>
</ul>
<p>기존 배열의 순서를 역전해 새로운 배열을 만들어 전달한다</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="tsx" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> sequence</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">sequence.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toReversed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// => [3, 2, 1]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">sequence; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// => [1, 2, 3]</span></span></code></pre></figure>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> outOfOrder</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Uint8Array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">sequence; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// => [1, 2, 3];</span></span></code></pre></figure>
