<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// result</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0: {status: 'fulfilled', value: 1}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1: {status: 'rejected', reason: Error: 에러 발생! at &#x3C;anonymous>:3:60 at i (https://www.notion.so/890-97af8b4e61b9aaf1ef78.js:1…}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2: {status: 'fulfilled', value: 3}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.race</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 가장 먼저 처리되는 프라미스의 결과(혹은 에러)를 반환합니다.(성공, 실패와 상관없음)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">race</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'에러 발생!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">])</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.any</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 가장 먼저 처리되는 프라미스의 결과를 반환합니다. 단, 실패 처리는 모든 Promise가 실패한 경우 AggregateError 발생시킨다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">any</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'에러 발생!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  ),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">])</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(data))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>논리 할당 연산자</li>
</ul>
<p>논리 할당 연산자는 논리 연산자와 할당 표현식을 결합한다. 두가지 새로운 연산자가 있다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Or Or Equals</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">   a   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">   b   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (연산 후)     </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">         |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">         |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">         |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 아래와 동일</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// And And Equals</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">   a   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">   b   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (연산 후)     </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">         |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">   false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">        |</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 아래와 동일</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> b);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>숫자 구분자</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1_000_000_000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1,000,000,000(10억)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> amount</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1_234_500</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1,234,500</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>WeakRef, FinalizationRegistry</li>
</ul>
<p><code>WeakRef</code> 객체에는 <em>target</em> 또는 <em>referent</em>라고 하는 객체에 대한 약한 참조가 포함된다. 객체에 대한 <em>약한 참조</em>는 가비지 컬렉터에서 객체를 회수하는 것을 방지하지 않는 참조이다.</p>
<p>FinalizationRegistry는 레지스트리에 등록된 객체가 <em>회수</em>(가비지 컬렉션)될 때 <em>정리 콜백</em>(<em>종료자</em>)을 호출하도록 요청하는 방법을 제공한다.</p>
<p>콜백을 전달하는 registry를 만든다. (GC 메모리 해제 → FinalizationRegistry callback 호출)</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> id</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"counter"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>&#x3C;/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#024C1A;--shiki-dark:#72F088">div</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">>;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> finalizer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> FinalizationRegistry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">args</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(args));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">class</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> Counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  constructor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // DOM 요소에 대한 약한 참조 기억</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.ref </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> WeakRef</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(element);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">    // this.ref1 = element; // 참조를 넘길경우 메모리 해제 X</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.timer) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.count </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> tick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      // 여전히 존재하는 경우 약한 참조에서 요소를 가져옵니다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> element</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.ref.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">deref</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">      //   const element1 = this.ref1; // 참조를 넘길경우 메모리 해제 X</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">      if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (element) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        element.textContent </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.count;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">        // 더 이상 존재하지 않는 요소</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">        console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'The element is gone.'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">stop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">        this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.ref </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">      }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">    tick</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.timer </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setInterval</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(tick, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  stop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.timer) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">      clearInterval</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.timer);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">      this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.timer </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Counter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getElementById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'counter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getElementById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'counter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">remove</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// element 삭제 시 WeakRef에 의해서 GC에 의해서 해제</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">finalizer.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">register</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(document.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getElementById</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'counter'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'GC Memory Release'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><a href="https://blog.shiren.dev/2021-08-30/">https://blog.shiren.dev/2021-08-30/</a></p>
<p><a href="https://runebook.dev/ko/docs/javascript/global_objects/weakref">https://runebook.dev/ko/docs/javascript/global_objects/weakref</a></p>
<h2 id="es2020es11">ES2020(ES11)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es2020es11"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<ul>
<li>globalThis</li>
</ul>
<p>예전에는 브라우저의 전역객체는 window였고 Node.js의 전역객체는 global이었습니다. 둘이 달라서 분기처리를 해줘야 했던 경우가 많았는데 이제는 globalThis라는 것으로 통일되었습니다. 물론 기존 window나 global도 존재합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 브라우저에서는</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">globalThis </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> window; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// true</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 노드에서는</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">globalThis </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> global; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// true</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>optional chaining</li>
</ul>
<p>자바스크립트에서 가장 많이 보는 에러가 cannot read property X of undefined 또는 cannot read property Y of undefined입니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 이를 방지하기 위해서</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (a) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (a.b) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(a.b.c);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 또는</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a.b </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> a.b.c);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// optional chaining a.b가 없는경우 undefined 리턴됨</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(a?.b?.c);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Nullish Coalescing Operator</li>
</ul>
<p>null이나 undefined일 때만 b를 반환합니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'A'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// A</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'A'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'A'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// A</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> ??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'A'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 0</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Dynamic Import</li>
</ul>
<p>파일 import를 동적으로 할 수 있게 되었습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> config </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> './config.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> config.age;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'./config.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// promise를 동적으로 리턴</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">config</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> config.age;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(config);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Promise.allSettled</li>
</ul>
<p>Promise.all()은 모든 작업이 성공(reslove)해야 실행되는 특징과 달리 Promise.allSettled()은 도중에 실패(reject)되더라도 모든 실행을 할 수 있습니다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> p1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p1, resolved"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> p2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p2, resolved"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> p3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p3, rejected"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">all</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([p1, p2, p3])</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">response</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">_</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(response)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"fulfilled"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p1, resolved"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"fulfilled"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p2, resolved"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">status</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"rejected"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reason</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">"p3, rejected"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">allSettled</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([p1, p2, p3])</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(err);</span></span>

<li>참고사이트</li>
</ul>
