<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">prev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> prev </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> next;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`prev plus next : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>디스트럭처링(Destructuring)</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Object Destructuring</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 비구조화 할당</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> settings</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  noti: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    follow: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    alerts: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    unfollow: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  color: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    theme: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'dark'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  noti</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">data</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'init'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">unfollow</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {},</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">  color</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">theme</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> settings;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(unfollow, theme, data);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Array Destructuring</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> days</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Mon'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Tue'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Wen'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Thu'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Fri'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Sat'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 이전</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Mon = days[0];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Tue = days[1];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Wen = days[2];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Thu = days[3];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Fri = days[4];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Sat = days[5];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// const Sun = days[6];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 이후</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Mon</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Tue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Wen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Thu</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Fri</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Sat</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">Sun</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Sun'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> days;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(Mon, Tue, Wen, Thu, Fri, Sat, Sun);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>함수 매개변수의 디폴트 값 설정</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'World'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`Hello, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}!`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">sayName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// "Hello, World!"</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> sayHello</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">aName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hello '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> aName;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">sayHi</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">());</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>기존 JS6이전에는 aName이 undefined 여부를 체크한 이후에 다시 값을 세팅해야했는데, 인자에 대한 초기값 세팅이 가능해졌다.
예) let defalutName = aName || "hong"</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Rest 파라미터, Spread 표현식</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Spread</p>
</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Spread object/Array unpack</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> alpha</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'a'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'b'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'c'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">number, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">alpha]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">//[1, 2, 3, 4, "a", "b", "c"]</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Rest</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> bestfriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">one</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">friendsRest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">one</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, friends rest : ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friendsRest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">bestfriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'kim'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'choi'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'seyoung'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Rest &#x26; Spread Destructure</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// object 삭제 &#x26; 정리할경우 유용</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  name: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  age: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">36</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  password: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'**123'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> ignorepwd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">password</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> rest;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">ignorepwd</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(user));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// object를 만들어서 return 할 경우 () 감싸줘야한다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> setCountry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">country</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'kr'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">rest</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> }) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ({ country, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">rest }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// ({country, ...rest}) spread 문법사용</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setCountry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(user));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>제너레이터(Generator)</li>
</ul>
<p>함수의 흐름을 특정 구간에 멈춰놓았다가 다시 실행할 수 있다.
결과값을 여러번 내보낼 수 있다.
Generator 문법이 나오면서 redux-saga, rsjx등 여러 라이브러리가 나오게 되었음</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> gen</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">yield</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 4</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(g.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">next</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// undefined</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// next를 수행하면서 각각 여러 동작들을 순서에 맞게 처리가능</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> listPeople</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">\</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> friendTeller</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> of</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friends) {</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>

<ul>
<li>프로미스(Promise)</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>create promises</li>
</ul>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'I am new Promise'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(newPromise);</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">setInterval</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(console.log, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, newPromise);</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>using promises</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'I am new Promise'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// resolve : then, reject : catch 호출이며 단 하나만 호출이 된다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">newPromise</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(value))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">catch</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`error ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>chaining promises</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> newPromise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">reject</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">resolve</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> timesTwo</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> number \</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(timesTwo)</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(timesTwo)</span></span>

<ul>
<li>promises all/race</li>
</ul>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'secode'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  setTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(resolve, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">7000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'third'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.all 시간과 상관없이 순서에 맞게 값을 제공해준다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> fall</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">all</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([f1, f2, f3]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">fall.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">values</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(values));</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Promise.race f1,f2,f3중 가장먼저 resolve, reject 되는 내용의 결과값을 제공해준다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> frace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> Promise</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">race</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([f1, f2, f3]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">fall.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">values</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(values));</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>모듈(ES Module)</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Named export</li>
</ul>
<p>Named export는 한 파일에서 여러 번 할 수 있다. Named export를 통해 내보낸 것 들은 추후 다른 모듈에서 내보낼 때와 같은 이름으로 import 해야 한다.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Park'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Ted'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> student3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Abby'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student3};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student, student2, student3} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {student </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> park, student2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> ted, student3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> abby} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> \</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> students </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'students.js'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Default export</li>
</ul>
<p>반면에 Default export는 한 스크립트 파일당 한 개만 사용할 수 있다. 그리고 export default의 뒤에는 표현식만 허용되므로 var, let, const등의 키워드는 사용하지 못한다.</p>
<p>이렇게 내보낸 객체들은 모듈들에서 접근할 수 있다. 그렇다면 지금부터는 모듈에서 export 한 객체들을 가져오는 import문을 살펴보자.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">export</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> default</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Jack'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> jack </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'studentJack'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es5-2009">ES5 (2009)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es5-2009"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>배열에 forEach, map, filter, reduce, some, every와 같은 메소드 지원
Object에 대한 getter / setter 지원</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> person </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  firstName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  lastName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'SeungAh'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fullName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.firstName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ' '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.lastName;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> fullName</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> name.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">toString</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">().</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">split</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">' '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.firstName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.lastName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">person.fullName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> 'Hong SeungAh'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(person.firstName); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Hong</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(person.lastName); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// SeungAh</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// defineProperty 속성추가가능</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> person </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  firstName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'Hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  lastName: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'SeungAH'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">defineProperty</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(person, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'fullName'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> () {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.firstName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ' '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.lastName;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">  set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">    var</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> name.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">split</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">' '</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.firstName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">    this</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">.lastName </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> words[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF"> ''</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">});</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h2 id="es3-1999">ES3 (1999)<a class="heading-anchor" aria-hidden="true" tabindex="-1" href="#es3-1999"><span class="icon icon-link"></span></a></h2>
<p>우리가 흔히 말하는 자바스크립트.
자바스크립트 strict 모드 지원 (더 깐깐한? 문법 검사를 한다.)
JSON 지원 ( 과거에는 XML을 사용하다가, json이 뜨면서 지원하게 됨 )</p>

<li>Array</li>

<ul>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 잘못된 length의 array에 접근시 undefined가 나오는 문제는 생긴다.(ex: i&#x3C;20)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> friends.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">]</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> myFriends</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#702C00;--shiki-dark:#FFB757">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> console.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">log</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">`my best friends ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}, ${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">friend</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">}`</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>

<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// uniquie함을 보장해준다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> info</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">Symbol</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)]: {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">    age: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">35</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  },</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">Symbol</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'hong'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">)]: {</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">  hello: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#032563;--shiki-dark:#ADDCFF">'bye'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// key에 대한 privacy 보장</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">keys</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(info);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">Object.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF">getOwnPropertySymbols</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">(info); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// private을 보장하지 않는다.</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>Sets</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast"><code data-language="javascript" data-theme="github-light-high-contrast github-dark-high-contrast" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// Sets</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// 중복된 값을 저장하지 않는다.</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF"> userset</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#A0111F;--shiki-dark:#FF9492"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#622CBC;--shiki-dark:#DBB7FF"> Set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">6</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#023B95;--shiki-dark:#91CBFF">8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0E1116;--shiki-dark:#F0F3F6">]);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#66707B;--shiki-dark:#BDC4CC">// weakset은 has,add,delete 정도만 가지고 있는 작은 set이다.</span></span>
